It sounds so real that I actually answer her out loud... before I realize nobody else heard it. \n\nI don't really like talking about it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had experiences where you believed something was true that others didn't seem to understand or agree with, even though you felt certain about it?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess. Just... at work. \n\nI know a couple of the other servers, and... this one regular customer... I know they're watching how I ring things in. They leave these little signs on the tickets. To prove they're trying to get me fired. \n\nMy husband says I'm wrong... he says it's just normal restaurant mess-ups. But... I know what they're doing. I'd rather not get into it, though.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like things in your environment—like signs, comments, or events—were specifically directed at you or meant to send you a hidden message?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess I just mentioned that. The tickets. \n\nLike I said... I just know they leave little signs on them. The other servers, and that one regular. It's... it's just little things on the receipts, or how they write them. To... to show they're watching me ring things in. Trying to get me fired. \n\nMy husband says it's just normal mistakes, but... I don't know. I just know what I see. I don't really want to talk about them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about how you know the signs on the tickets are meant for you? Do you feel like you're figuring it out on your own, or do you feel like someone is helping you understand them?\n\nPatient: I... I just figure it out myself. I already told you about it. \n\nI just... I look at the tickets, and I can tell. The way the servers write things, or... that regular customer. It's just obvious they're watching me ring things in. They leave those little signs to prove they're trying to get me fired. \n\nMy husband keeps saying it's just normal mess-ups, but... he's not there. I am. I don't... I don't know why we have to keep going over this. I just know it's happening.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been experiencing these things, like hearing the voice and seeing signs on the tickets?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know exactly. A few months, I guess. \n\nMaybe... about four months since I really started noticing the tickets and... and being sure about what they're doing. And the voice getting clearer. Since then, mostly.\n\nDoctor: Outside of work, have you ever experienced any strange thoughts, beliefs, or perceptions that others don't seem to share, even when you were certain they were true?\n\nPatient: No. Just... just at work. That's it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced any strange thoughts, voices, or perceptions when you’re not at work—like hearing voices or seeing things that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: I... well, I guess sometimes when I'm home. Just in the kitchen. \n\nI'll hear... a woman. She says my name, or tells me I'm doing the count wrong. It's clear enough that I actually answer her out loud. But then I realize no one else heard it. So... I guess it happens at home sometimes too. But it's still mostly just... work stuff.\n\nDoctor: When you hear the woman's voice at home, do you ever feel like it's part of something bigger—like it's trying to warn you about something or communicating a special message just for you?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I'd rather not get into it, though.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like things in your environment—like signs, comments, or events—were specifically directed at you or meant to send you a hidden message?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess I just mentioned that. The tickets. \n\nLike I said... I just know they leave little signs on them. The other servers, and that one regular. It's... it's just little things on the receipts, or how they write them. That's it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced any strange thoughts, voices, or perceptions when you’re not at work—like hearing voices or seeing things that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: I... well, I guess sometimes when I'm home. Just in the kitchen. \n\nI'll hear... a woman. She says my name, or tells me I'm doing the count wrong. It's clear enough that I actually answer her out loud. But then I realize no one else heard it. To prove they're trying to get me fired. \n\nMy husband says I'm wrong... he says it's just normal restaurant mess-ups. But... I know what they're doing. I'd rather not get into it, though.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like things in your environment—like signs, comments, or events—were specifically directed at you or meant to send you a hidden message?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess I just mentioned that. The tickets. \n\nLike I said... I just know they leave little signs on them. The other servers, and that one regular. It's... it's just little things on the receipts, or how they write them. To... to show they're watching me ring things in. Trying to get me fired. \n\nMy husband says it's just normal mistakes, but... I don't know. I just know what I see. I don't really want to talk about them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about how you know the signs on the tickets are meant for you? Do you feel like you're figuring it out on your own, or do you feel like someone is helping you understand them?\n\nPatient: I... I just figure it out myself. I already told you about it. \n\nI just... I look at the tickets, and I can tell. The way the servers write things, or... that regular customer. It's just obvious they're watching me ring things in. They leave those little signs to prove they're trying to get me fired. \n\nMy husband keeps saying it's just normal mess-ups, but... he's not there. I am. I don't... I don't know why we have to keep going over this. I just know it's happening.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been experiencing these things, like hearing the voice and seeing signs on the tickets?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know exactly. Since then, mostly.\n\nDoctor: Outside of work, have you ever experienced any strange thoughts, beliefs, or perceptions that others don't seem to share, even when you were certain they were true?\n\nPatient: No. Just... just at work. That's it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced any strange thoughts, voices, or perceptions when you’re not at work—like hearing voices or seeing things that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: I... well, I guess sometimes when I'm home. So... I guess it happens at home sometimes too.
